  continue\\n            layer = layers_module[i]\\n            hook = layer.register_forward_hook(self._make_hook(i))\\n            self._hooks.append(hook)\\n        log.info(\\\"Hooked %d layers: %s\\\", len(self._hooks), \\n                [i for i in PALIGEMMA_LAYERS if i < n_layers])\\n\\n    def _make_hook(self, layer_idx):\\n        def hook_fn(_module, _input, output):\\n            h = output[0] if isinstance(output, tuple) else output\\n            self._activations[layer_idx] = h[:, 0].detach().float().cpu()\\n        return hook_fn\\n\\n    def get(self):\\n        if not self._activations:\\n            return None\\n        parts = []\\n        for i in PALIGEMMA_LAYERS:\\n            if i in self._activations:\\n                parts.append(self._activations[i].squeeze(0))\\n        self._activations.clear()\\n        if not parts:\\n            return None\\n        return torch.cat(parts, dim=-1)\\n\\n    def remove(self):\\n        for h in self._hooks:\\n            h.remove()\\n        self._hooks.clear()\\n\\n\\ndef collect_from_perturbation(policy, collector, task_suite, perturbation_type,\\n shuffled_tasks = list(task_ids)\\n    rng.shuffle(shuffled_tasks)\\n\\n    for task_id in shuffled_tasks:\\n        if env_count >= num_envs:\\n            break\\n        if not isinstance(task_id, has_view_encoding = \\\"_view_\\\" in task_name and \\\"_initstate_\\\" in task_name\\n        if has_view_encoding:\\n            bddl_path = str(bddl_root / task_suite / f\\\"{task_name}.bddl\\\")\\n            base_bddl = bddl_root / task_suite / f\\\"\\n\\n... parser.add_argument(\\\"--num_envs_per_perturbation\\\", type=int, default=40)\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--steps_per_env\\\", type=int, default=8)\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--seed\\\", type=int, default=42)\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--checkpoint\\\", default=\\\"/root/autodl-tmp/checkpoints/pi0_libero_openpi\\\")\\n    args = parser.parse_args()\\n\\n    device = args.device\\n    os.makedirs(args.output_dir, exist_ok=True)\\n\\n    log.info(\\\"Loading policy on %s...\\\", device)\\n    from openpi_metabrain_policy import OpenPIMetaBrainPolicy\\n    policy_wrapper = policy_wrapper._diag_max = 0\\n\\n    collector = MultiLayerCollector(policy_wrapper.model)\\n    collector.register()\\n\\n    # Sanity check\\n    from evaluate_libero_plus import obs_to_model_input\\n    dummy_obs = {\\n        \\\"agentview_image\\\": np.zeros((224, 224, 3), dtype=np.uint8),\\n        \\\"robot0_eye_in_hand_image\\\": np.zeros((224, 224, 3),  \\\"background_textures\\\", \\\"camera_viewpoints\\\", \\\"light_conditions\\\", \\\"sensor_noise\\\",\\n        \\\"objects_layout\\\", \\\"robot_initial_states\\\",\\n    ]\\n    visual_envs = args.num_envs_per_perturbation\\n    other_envs = args.num_envs_per_perturbation * 3\\n    samples_config = {\\n        \\\"background_textures\\\": visual_envs,\\n        \\\"camera_viewpoints\\\": visual_envs,\\n        \\\"light_conditions\\\": visual_envs,\\n        \\\"sensor_noise\\\": visual_envs,\\n        \\\"objects_layout\\\": other_envs,\\n        \\\"robot_initial_states\\\": other_envs,\\n    }\\n\\n    all_activations = []\\n    all_metadata = []\\n\\n    for ptype in perturbation_types:\\n        n_envs = samples_config[ptype]\\n        log.info(\\\"Collecting from %s all_activations.extend(acts)\\n        all_metadata.extend(meta)\\n\\n    if not all_activations:\\n        log.error(\\\"No activations collected!\\\")\\n        return\\n\\n    X = {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_014eyT6vQfVKvirMTjTZ8eeQ","type":"tool_result","content":"{\"result\":\"{\\\"success\\\": true, \\\"data\\\": {\\\"stdout\\\": \\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\nTrain PAR Router on real LIBERO-Plus activations (3-class: visual/spatial/physical).\\\\n\\\\nInput: activations.pt from collect_activations_real_env.py\\\\nOutput: PAR router checkpoint compatible with phase1/par_router.py\\\\n\\\\n5-fold stratified cross-validation, reports macro F1.\\\\n\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\nimport argparse\\\\nimport json\\\\nimport logging\\\\nimport os\\\\nfrom pathlib import Path\\\\n\\\\nimport numpy as np\\\\nimport torch\\\\nimport torch.nn as nn\\\\nimport torch.optim as optim\\\\nfrom sklearn.metrics import f1_score, classification_report\\\\nfrom sklearn.model_selection import StratifiedKFold\\\\nfrom torch.utils.data import DataLoader, TensorDataset, Subset\\\\n\\\\nlogging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO, format=\\\\\\\"%(asctime)s %(levelname)s %(message)s\\\\\\\")\\\\nlog = logging.getLogger(__name__)\\\\n\\\\nCLASS_NAMES = [\\\\\\\"visual\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"spatial\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"physical\\\\\\\"]\\\\n\\\\n\\\\nclass PARRouterModel(nn.Module):\\\\n    def __init__(self, input_dim=2048, hidden_dim=256, num_classes=3, dropout=0.1):\\\\n        super().__init__()\\\\n        self.net = nn.Sequential(\\\\n            nn.Linear(input_dim, hidden_dim),\\\\n            nn.ReLU(),\\\\n            nn.Dropout(dropout),\\\\n            nn.Linear(hidden_dim, num_classes),\\\\n        )\\\\n\\\\n    def forward(self, x):\\\\n        return self.net(x)\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef train_one_fold(X_train, y_train, X_val, y_val, input_dim, num_classes,\\\\n                   lr, epochs, device, hidden_dim=256, dropout=0.1):\\\\n    model = PARRouterModel(input_dim, hidden_dim, num_classes, dropout).to(device)\\\\n    optimizer = optim.AdamW(model.parameters(), lr=lr, weight_decay=1e-4)\\\\n    scheduler = optim.lr_scheduler.CosineAnnealingLR(optimizer, T_max=epochs)\\\\n    criterion = nn.CrossEntropyLoss()\\\\n\\\\n    train_ds = TensorDataset(X_train.to(device), y_train.to(device))\\\\n    train_loader = DataLoader(train_ds, batch_size=128, shuffle=True)\\\\n\\\\n    best_f1 = 0.0\\\\n    best_state = None\\\\n\\\\n    for epoch in range(epochs):\\\\n        model.train()\\\\n        for xb, yb in train_loader:\\\\n            optimizer.zero_grad()\\\\n            loss = criterion(model(xb), yb)\\\\n            loss.backward()\\\\n            optimizer.step()\\\\n        scheduler.step()\\\\n\\\\n        model.eval()\\\\n        with torch.no_grad():\\\\n            logits = model(X_val.to(device))\\\\n            preds = logits.argmax(dim=-1).cpu().numpy()\\\\n            y_true = y_val.numpy()\\\\n            f1 = f1_score(y_true, preds, average=\\\\\\\"macro\\\\\\\")\\\\n\\\\n        if f1 > best_f1:\\\\n            best_f1 = f1\\\\n            best_state = {k: v.cpu().clone() for k, v in model.state_dict().items()}\\\\n\\\\n    return best_f1, best_state\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef cross_validate(X, y, input_dim, num_classes, lr, epochs, device,\\\\n                   n_folds=5, hidden_dim=256, dropout=0.1):\\\\n    skf = StratifiedKFold(n_splits=n_folds, shuffle=True, random_state=42)\\\\n    fold_f1s = []\\\\n    best_overall_f1 = 0.0\\\\n    best_overall_state = None\\\\n\\\\n    for fold, (train_idx, val_idx) in enumerate(skf.split(X, y)):\\\\n        X_train, X_val = X[train_idx], X[val_idx]\\\\n        y_train, y_val = y[train_idx], y[val_idx]\\\\n\\\\n        f1, state = train_one_fold(\\\\n            X_train, y_train, X_val, y_val,\\\\n            input_dim, num_classes, lr, epochs, device, hidden_dim, dropout\\\\n        )\\\\n        fold_f1s.append(f1)\\\\n        log.info(\\\\\\\"  Fold %d: F1=%.4f\\\\\\\", fold + 1, f1)\\\\n\\\\n        if f1 > best_overall_f1:\\\\n            best_overall_f1 = f1\\\\n            best_overall_state = state\\\\n\\\\n    mean_f1 = np.mean(fold_f1s)\\\\n    std_f1 = np.std(fold_f1s)\\\\n    log.info(\\\\\\\"  CV: F1=%.4f +/- %.4f\\\\\\\", mean_f1, std_f1)\\\\n    return mean_f1, std_f1, fold_f1s, best_overall_state\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef main():\\\\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()\\\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\\\\\"--data_path\\\\\\\", default=\\\\\\\"/root/autodl-tmp/activations_real_env/activations.pt\\\\\\\")\\\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\\\\\"--output_dir\\\\\\\", default=\\\\\\\"/root/autodl-tmp/checkpoints/par_router_real_env\\\\\\\")\\\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\\\\\"--device\\\\\\\", default=\\\\\\\"cuda:2\\\\\\\")\\\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\\\\\"--lr\\\\\\\", type=float, default=1e-3)\\\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\\\\\"--epochs\\\\\\\", type=int, default=100)\\\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\\\\\"--hidden_dim\\\\\\\", type=int, default=256)\\\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\\\\\"--dropout\\\\\\\", type=float, default=0.1)\\\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\\\\\"--normalize\\\\\\\", action=\\\\\\\"store_true\\\\\\\")\\\\n    args = parser.parse_args()\\\\n\\\\n    device = args.device\\\\n    os.makedirs(args.output_dir, exist_ok=True)\\\\n\\\\n    log.info(\\\\\\\"Loading activations from %s\\\\\\\", args.data_path)\\\\n    data = torch.load(args.data_path, weights_only=False)\\\\n    X = data[\\\\\\\"activations\\\\\\\"]\\\\n    y = data[\\\\\\\"labels_3class\\\\\\\"]\\\\n\\\\n    log.info(\\\\\\\"Data: X=%s, y=%s\\\\\\\", X.shape, y.shape)\\\\n    log.info(\\\\\\\"Class counts: %s\\\\\\\", {CLASS_NAMES[i]: (y == i).sum().item() for i in range(3)})\\\\n    log.info(\\\\\\\"Mean activation norm: %.1f\\\\\\\", X.norm(dim=-1).mean().item())\\\\n\\\\n    if args.normalize:\\\\n        log.info(\\\\\\\"Applying L2 normalization\\\\\\\")\\\\n        X = torch.nn.functional.normalize(X, dim=-1)\\\\n\\\\n    input_dim = X.shape[-1]\\\\n    num_classes = 3\\\\n\\\\n    # Try multiple learning rates\\\\n    lrs = [1e-4, 5e-4, 1e-3, 3e-3]\\\\n    best_result = None\\\\n\\\\n    for lr in lrs:\\\\n        log.info(\\\\\\\"Training with lr=%.1e\\\\\\\", lr)\\\\n        mean_f1, std_f1, fold_f1s, best_state = cross_validate(\\\\n            X, y, input_dim, num_classes, lr, args.epochs, device,\\\\n            hidden_dim=args.hidden_dim, dropout=args.dropout\\\\n        )\\\\n        if best_result is None or mean_f1 > best_result[\\\\\\\"mean_f1\\\\\\\"]:\\\\n            best_result = {\\\\n                \\\\\\\"mean_f1\\\\\\\": mean_f1,\\\\n                \\\\\\\"std_f1\\\\\\\": std_f1,\\\\n                \\\\\\\"fold_f1s\\\\\\\": fold_f1s,\\\\n                \\\\\\\"lr\\\\\\\": lr,\\\\n                \\\\\\\"state\\\\\\\": best_state,\\\\n            }\\\\n\\\\n    log.info(\\\\\\\"Best: lr=%.1e, F1=%.4f +/- %.4f\\\\\\\", \\\\n             best_result[\\\\\\\"lr\\\\\\\"], best_result[\\\\\\\"mean_f1\\\\\\\"], best_result[\\\\\\\"std_f1\\\\\\\"])\\\\n\\\\n    # Save checkpoint (compatible with par_router.py)\\\\n    config = {\\\\n        \\\\\\\"input_dim\\\\\\\": input_dim,\\\\n        \\\\\\\"hidden_dim\\\\\\\": args.hidden_dim,\\\\n        \\\\\\\"num_classes\\\\\\\": num_classes,\\\\n        \\\\\\\"dropout\\\\\\\": args.dropout,\\\\n        \\\\\\\"layer\\\\\\\": \\\\\\\"paligemma_layer_6\\\\\\\",\\\\n        \\\\\\\"pooling\\\\\\\": \\\\\\\"first\\\\\\\",\\\\n        \\\\\\\"lr\\\\\\\": best_result[\\\\\\\"lr\\\\\\\"],\\\\n        \\\\\\\"normalize\\\\\\\": args.normalize,\\\\n    }\\\\n\\\\n    ckpt_path = Path(args.output_dir) / \\\\\\\"best_router.pt\\\\\\\"\\\\n    torch.save({\\\\n        \\\\\\\"model_state_dict\\\\\\\": best_result[\\\\\\\"state\\\\\\\"],\\\\n        \\\\\\\"config\\\\\\\": config,\\\\n        \\\\\\\"cv_results\\\\\\\": {\\\\n            \\\\\\\"mean_f1\\\\\\\": best_result[\\\\\\\"mean_f1\\\\\\\"],\\\\n            \\\\\\\"std_f1\\\\\\\": best_result[\\\\\\\"std_f1\\\\\\\"],\\\\n            \\\\\\\"fold_f1s\\\\\\\": best_result[\\\\\\\"fold_f1s\\\\\\\"],\\\\n        },\\\\n        \\\\\\\"data_info\\\\\\\": {\\\\n            \\\\\\\"source\\\\\\\": str(args.data_path),\\\\n            \\\\\\\"num_samples\\\\\\\": len(X),\\\\n            \\\\\\\"class_counts\\\\\\\": {CLASS_NAMES[i]: (y == i).sum().item() for i in range(3)},\\\\n        }\\\\n    }, ckpt_path)\\\\n    log.info(\\\\\\\"Router checkpoint saved to %s\\\\\\\", ckpt_path)\\\\n\\\\n    # Also save result JSON\\\\n    result_path = Path(args.output_dir) / \\\\\\\"training_result.json\\\\\\\"\\\\n    with open(result_path, \\\\\\\"w\\\\\\\") as f:\\\\n {\\\"elapsed_seconds\\\": 0.43}}\"}","structuredContent":{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"\\\"\\\"\\\"\\nTrain PAR Router on real LIBERO-Plus activations (3-class: visual/spatial/physical).\\n\\nInput: activations.pt from collect_activations_real_env.py\\nOutput: PAR router checkpoint compatible with phase1/par_router.py\\n\\n5-fold stratified cross-validation, reports macro F1.\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\nimport argparse\\nimport json\\nimport logging\\nimport os\\nfrom pathlib import Path\\n\\nimport numpy as np\\nimport torch\\nimport torch.nn as nn\\nimport torch.optim as optim\\nfrom sklearn.metrics import f1_score, classification_report\\nfrom sklearn.model_selection import StratifiedKFold\\nfrom torch.utils.data import DataLoader, TensorDataset, Subset\\n\\nlogging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO, format=\\\"%(asctime)s %(levelname)s %(message)s\\\")\\nlog = logging.getLogger(__name__)\\n\\nCLASS_NAMES = [\\\"visual\\\", \\\"spatial\\\", \\\"physical\\\"]\\n\\n\\nclass PARRouterModel(nn.Module):\\n    def __init__(self, input_dim=2048, hidden_dim=256, num_classes=3, dropout=0.1):\\n        super().__init__()\\n        self.net = nn.Sequential(\\n            nn.Linear(input_dim, hidden_dim),\\n            nn.ReLU(),\\n            nn.Dropout(dropout),\\n            nn.Linear(hidden_dim, num_classes),\\n        )\\n\\n    def forward(self, x):\\n        return self.net(x)\\n\\n\\ndef train_one_fold(X_train, y_train, X_val, y_val, input_dim, num_classes,\\n                   lr, epochs, device, hidden_dim=256, dropout=0.1):\\n    model = PARRouterModel(input_dim, hidden_dim, num_classes, dropout).to(device)\\n    optimizer = optim.AdamW(model.parameters(), lr=lr, weight_decay=1e-4)\\n    scheduler = optim.lr_scheduler.CosineAnnealingLR(optimizer, T_max=epochs)\\n    criterion = nn.CrossEntropyLoss()\\n\\n    train_ds = TensorDataset(X_train.to(device), y_train.to(device))\\n    train_loader = DataLoader(train_ds, batch_size=128, shuffle=True)\\n\\n    best_f1 = 0.0\\n    best_state = None\\n\\n    for epoch in range(epochs):\\n        model.train()\\n        for xb, yb in train_loader:\\n            optimizer.zero_grad()\\n            loss = criterion(model(xb), yb)\\n            loss.backward()\\n            optimizer.step()\\n        scheduler.step()\\n\\n        model.eval()\\n        with torch.no_grad():\\n            logits = model(X_val.to(device))\\n            preds = logits.argmax(dim=-1).cpu().numpy()\\n            y_true = y_val.numpy()\\n            f1 = f1_score(y_true, preds, average=\\\"macro\\\")\\n\\n        if f1 > best_f1:\\n            best_f1 = f1\\n            best_state = {k: v.cpu().clone() for k, v in model.state_dict().items()}\\n\\n    return best_f1, best_state\\n\\n\\ndef cross_validate(X, y, input_dim, num_classes, lr, epochs, device,\\n                   n_folds=5, hidden_dim=256, dropout=0.1):\\n    skf = StratifiedKFold(n_splits=n_folds, shuffle=True, random_state=42)\\n    fold_f1s = []\\n    best_overall_f1 = 0.0\\n    best_overall_state = None\\n\\n    for fold, (train_idx, val_idx) in enumerate(skf.split(X, y)):\\n        X_train, X_val = X[train_idx], X[val_idx]\\n        y_train, y_val = y[train_idx], y[val_idx]\\n\\n        f1, state = train_one_fold(\\n            X_train, y_train, X_val, y_val,\\n            input_dim, num_classes, lr, epochs, device, hidden_dim, dropout\\n        )\\n        fold_f1s.append(f1)\\n        log.info(\\\"  Fold %d: F1=%.4f\\\", fold + 1, f1)\\n\\n        if f1 > best_overall_f1:\\n            best_overall_f1 = f1\\n            best_overall_state = state\\n\\n    mean_f1 = np.mean(fold_f1s)\\n    std_f1 = np.std(fold_f1s)\\n    log.info(\\\"  CV: F1=%.4f +/- %.4f\\\", mean_f1, std_f1)\\n    return mean_f1, std_f1, fold_f1s, best_overall_state\\n\\n\\ndef main():\\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--data_path\\\", default=\\\"/root/autodl-tmp/activations_real_env/activations.pt\\\")\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--output_dir\\\", default=\\\"/root/autodl-tmp/checkpoints/par_router_real_env\\\")\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--device\\\", default=\\\"cuda:2\\\")\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--lr\\\", type=float, default=1e-3)\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--epochs\\\", type=int, default=100)\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--hidden_dim\\\", type=int, default=256)\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--dropout\\\", type=float, default=0.1)\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--normalize\\\", action=\\\"store_true\\\")\\n    args = parser.parse_args()\\n\\n    device = args.device\\n    os.makedirs(args.output_dir, exist_ok=True)\\n\\n    log.info(\\\"Loading activations from %s\\\", args.data_path)\\n    data = torch.load(args.data_path, weights_only=False)\\n    X = data[\\\"activations\\\"]\\n    y = data[\\\"labels_3class\\\"]\\n\\n    log.info(\\\"Data: X=%s, y=%s\\\", X.shape, y.shape)\\n    log.info(\\\"Class counts: %s\\\", {CLASS_NAMES[i]: (y == i).sum().item() for i in range(3)})\\n    log.info(\\\"Mean activation norm: %.1f\\\", X.norm(dim=-1).mean().item())\\n\\n    if args.normalize:\\n        log.info(\\\"Applying L2 normalization\\\")\\n        X = torch.nn.functional.normalize(X, dim=-1)\\n\\n    input_dim = X.shape[-1]\\n    num_classes = 3\\n\\n    # Try multiple learning rates\\n    lrs = [1e-4, 5e-4, 1e-3, 3e-3]\\n    best_result = None\\n\\n    for lr in lrs:\\n        log.info(\\\"Training with lr=%.1e\\\", lr)\\n        mean_f1, std_f1, fold_f1s, best_state = cross_validate(\\n            X, y, input_dim, num_classes, lr, args.epochs, device,\\n            hidden_dim=args.hidden_dim, dropout=args.dropout\\n        )\\n        if best_result is None or mean_f1 > best_result[\\\"mean_f1\\\"]:\\n            best_result = {\\n                \\\"mean_f1\\\": mean_f1,\\n                \\\"std_f1\\\": std_f1,\\n                \\\"fold_f1s\\\": fold_f1s,\\n                \\\"lr\\\": lr,\\n                \\\"state\\\": best_state,\\n            }\\n\\n    log.info(\\\"Best: lr=%.1e, F1=%.4f +/- %.4f\\\", \\n             best_result[\\\"lr\\\"], best_result[\\\"mean_f1\\\"], best_result[\\\"std_f1\\\"])\\n\\n    # Save checkpoint (compatible with par_router.py)\\n    config = {\\n        \\\"input_dim\\\": input_dim,\\n        \\\"hidden_dim\\\": args.hidden_dim,\\n        \\\"num_classes\\\": num_classes,\\n        \\\"dropout\\\": args.dropout,\\n        \\\"layer\\\": \\\"paligemma_layer_6\\\",\\n        \\\"pooling\\\": \\\"first\\\",\\n        \\\"lr\\\": best_result[\\\"lr\\\"],\\n        \\\"normalize\\\": args.normalize,\\n    }\\n\\n    ckpt_path = Path(args.output_dir) / \\\"best_router.pt\\\"\\n    torch.save({\\n        \\\"model_state_dict\\\": best_result[\\\"state\\\"],\\n        \\\"config\\\": config,\\n        \\\"cv_results\\\": {\\n            \\\"mean_f1\\\": best_result[\\\"mean_f1\\\"],\\n            \\\"std_f1\\\": best_result[\\\"std_f1\\\"],\\n            \\\"fold_f1s\\\": best_result[\\\"fold_f1s\\\"],\\n        },\\n        \\\"data_info\\\": {\\n            \\\"source\\\": str(args.data_path),\\n            \\\"num_samples\\\": len(X),\\n            \\\"class_counts\\\": {CLASS_NAMES[i]: (y == i).sum().item() for i in range(3)},\\n        }\\n    }, ckpt_path)\\n    log.info(\\\"Router checkpoint saved to %s\\\", ckpt_path)\\n\\n    # Also save result JSON\\n    result_path = Path(args.output_dir) / \\\"training_result.json\\\"\\n    with open(result_path, \\\"w\\\") as f:\\n        json.dump({\\n            \\\"mean_f1\\\": best_result[\\\"mean_f1\\\"],\\n            \\\"std_f1\\\": best_result[\\\"std_f1\\\"],\\n            \\\"fold_f1s\\\": best_result[\\\"fold_f1s\\\"],\\n            \\\"best_lr\\\": best_result[\\\"lr\\\"],\\n            \\\"config\\\": config,\\n            \\\"data_info\\\": {\\n                \\\"source\\\": str(args.data_path),\\n                \\\"num_samples\\\": len(X),\\n                \\\"class_counts\\\": {CLASS_NAMES[i]: (y == i).sum().item() for i in range(3)},\\n            }\\n        }, f, indent=2)\\n    log.info(\\\"Result saved to %s\\\",
